\glsadd{AE-data-bus}\dictentry{Data Bus}{A communication network for aircraft systems.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-arinc-429}\dictentry{ARINC 429}{A standard for avionics data communication.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-mil-std-1553}\dictentry{MIL-STD-1553}{A military standard for data bus communication.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-aircraft-wiring}\dictentry{Aircraft Wiring}{Electrical wiring in aircraft systems.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-circuit-breaker}\dictentry{Circuit Breaker}{A resettable overcurrent protection device.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-bus-bar}\dictentry{Bus Bar}{A power distribution conductor.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-generator}\dictentry{Generator}{A device converting mechanical to electrical power.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-alternator}\dictentry{Alternator}{An AC generator in aircraft electrical systems.}{catAE}
\glsadd{SS-battery}\dictentry{Battery}{An electrochemical power storage device.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-apu}\dictentry{APU}{Auxiliary Power Unit, a small engine for ground power.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-inverter}\dictentry{Inverter}{A device converting DC to AC power.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-transformer}\dictentry{Transformer}{A device changing AC voltage levels.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-rectifier}\dictentry{Rectifier}{A device converting AC to DC.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-voltage-regulator}\dictentry{Voltage Regulator}{A device maintaining constant voltage.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-oxygen-system}\dictentry{Oxygen System}{A system providing supplemental oxygen.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-pressurization-system}\dictentry{Pressurization System}{A system maintaining cabin pressure.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-air-conditioning}\dictentry{Air Conditioning}{A system controlling cabin temperature.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-bleed-air}\dictentry{Bleed Air}{Compressed air from the engine for pneumatic systems.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-ecs-pack}\dictentry{ECS Pack}{An environmental control system unit.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-ventilation}\dictentry{Ventilation}{Cabin air circulation system.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-rotor-brake}\dictentry{Rotor Brake}{A brake stopping the rotor after shutdown.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-rotor-head}\dictentry{Rotor Head}{The assembly connecting rotor blades to the mast.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-swashplate}\dictentry{Swashplate}{A mechanism translating controls to blade pitch.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-collective-pitch}\dictentry{Collective Pitch}{Simultaneous pitch change of all rotor blades.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-cyclic-pitch}\dictentry{Cyclic Pitch}{Cyclic variation of blade pitch for rotor tilt.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-lead-lag-hinge}\dictentry{Lead-Lag Hinge}{A hinge allowing blade fore-aft movement.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-flapping-hinge}\dictentry{Flapping Hinge}{A hinge allowing blade vertical movement.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-feathering-hinge}\dictentry{Feathering Hinge}{A hinge allowing blade pitch change.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-fully-articulated-rotor}\dictentry{Fully Articulated Rotor}{A rotor with all three hinges.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-semi-rigid-rotor}\dictentry{Semi-Rigid Rotor}{A rotor with a teetering hinge.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-rigid-rotor}\dictentry{Rigid Rotor}{A rotor without hinges, relying on blade flexibility.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-tail-rotor}\dictentry{Tail Rotor}{A rotor counteracting main rotor torque.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-fenestron}\dictentry{Fenestron}{A ducted tail rotor.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-notar}\dictentry{NOTAR}{No tail rotor, using circulation control.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-main-rotor}\dictentry{Main Rotor}{The primary lifting rotor of a helicopter.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-tip-speed}\dictentry{Tip Speed}{The speed of the rotor blade tip.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-hover}\dictentry{Hover}{Flight at zero ground speed.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-autorotation-landing}\dictentry{Autorotation Landing}{A landing without engine power.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-ground-resonance}\dictentry{Ground Resonance}{A destructive oscillation of rotor and airframe.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-retreating-blade-stall}\dictentry{Retreating Blade Stall}{Stall of the retreating rotor blade at high speed.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-dissymmetry-of-lift}\dictentry{Dissymmetry of Lift}{Unequal lift between advancing and retreating blades.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-translational-lift}\dictentry{Translational Lift}{Lift increase with forward speed.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-effective-translational-lift}\dictentry{Effective Translational Lift}{Lift increase as the rotor leaves its downwash.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-ground-effect-helicopter}\dictentry{Ground Effect Helicopter}{Increased lift near the ground.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-vortex-ring-state}\dictentry{Vortex Ring State}{A dangerous descent condition for helicopters.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-settling-with-power}\dictentry{Settling With Power}{Another term for vortex ring state.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-power-required-curve}\dictentry{Power Required Curve}{A curve showing power needed for flight.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-power-available-curve}\dictentry{Power Available Curve}{A curve showing power available from the engine.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-height-velocity-diagram}\dictentry{Height-Velocity Diagram}{A diagram showing safe hover and landing regions.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-h-v-curve}\dictentry{H-V Curve}{The height-velocity diagram for helicopters.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-cat-a-takeoff}\dictentry{Cat A Takeoff}{A takeoff with one engine failure capability.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-cat-b-takeoff}\dictentry{Cat B Takeoff}{A takeoff assuming no engine failure.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-stall-warning-system}\dictentry{Stall Warning System}{A system alerting the pilot of an impending stall condition.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-stick-shaker}\dictentry{Stick Shaker}{A device vibrating the control column to indicate an approaching stall.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-yaw-damper}\dictentry{Yaw Damper}{An automatic flight control system damping Dutch roll oscillations.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-autothrottle}\dictentry{Autothrottle}{An automatic system controlling engine thrust to maintain speed.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-flight-director}\dictentry{Flight Director}{A flight instrument displaying command guidance cues to the pilot.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-tcas}\dictentry{TCAS}{Traffic Alert and Collision Avoidance System for preventing mid-air collisions.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-gpws}\dictentry{GPWS}{Ground Proximity Warning System alerting the crew of terrain hazards.}{catAE}
\glsadd{AE-radar-altimeter}\dictentry{Radar Altimeter}{An instrument measuring altitude using radio wave reflection.}{catAE}
\glsadd{SS-transponder}\dictentry{Transponder}{An aircraft transceiver for ATC identification and altitude reporting.}{catAE}
\end{multicols}

\clearpage
\chapter*{Rockets and Missiles}
\addcontentsline{toc}{chapter}{Rockets and Missiles}
\markboth{Rockets and Missiles}{Rockets and Missiles}
\clearpage
\begin{multicols}{2}
\small
Rocket propulsion, launch vehicle design, and guided missile systems. From the Tsiolkovsky equation to terminal guidance and hypersonic glide vehicles.\par
\vspace{0.5cm}
\glsadd{RM-rocket-engine}\dictentry{Rocket Engine}{A thrust chamber where propellant is burned or heated and expelled.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-solid-rocket-motor}\dictentry{Solid Rocket Motor}{A rocket using solid chemical propellant cast in a case.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-liquid-rocket-engine}\dictentry{Liquid Rocket Engine}{An engine using liquid fuel and oxidizer pumped to the combustion chamber.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-hybrid-rocket}\dictentry{Hybrid Rocket}{A rocket combining solid fuel with liquid or gaseous oxidizer.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-specific-impulse}\dictentry{Specific Impulse}{A measure of rocket propellant efficiency.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-total-impulse}\dictentry{Total Impulse}{The total thrust integrated over burn time.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-thrust}\dictentry{Thrust}{The force propelling a rocket forward.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-thrust-to-weight-ratio}\dictentry{Thrust-to-Weight Ratio}{The ratio of engine thrust to vehicle weight.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-propellant}\dictentry{Propellant}{The fuel and oxidizer mixture burned in a rocket engine.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-oxidizer}\dictentry{Oxidizer}{The substance supplying oxygen for combustion in a rocket.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-bipropellant}\dictentry{Bipropellant}{A rocket using separate fuel and oxidizer.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-monopropellant}\dictentry{Monopropellant}{A single propellant decomposed by a catalyst.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-hypergolic}\dictentry{Hypergolic}{A propellant combination that ignites on contact.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-cryogenic-propellant}\dictentry{Cryogenic Propellant}{Propellant stored at extremely low temperatures.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-rp-1}\dictentry{RP-1}{A refined kerosene used as rocket fuel.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-liquid-hydrogen}\dictentry{Liquid Hydrogen}{A cryogenic fuel used in high-performance rockets.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-liquid-oxygen}\dictentry{Liquid Oxygen}{A cryogenic oxidizer used with hydrogen and kerosene.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-nitrogen-tetroxide}\dictentry{Nitrogen Tetroxide}{A storable oxidizer for hypergolic propellants.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-hydrazine}\dictentry{Hydrazine}{A storable monopropellant and fuel.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-mmh}\dictentry{MMH}{Monomethylhydrazine, a hypergolic fuel.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-udmh}\dictentry{UDMH}{Unsymmetrical dimethylhydrazine, a hypergolic fuel.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-propellant-tank}\dictentry{Propellant Tank}{A tank storing rocket propellant.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-tank-pressurization}\dictentry{Tank Pressurization}{Pressurizing tanks to feed propellant to the engine.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-combustion-chamber}\dictentry{Combustion Chamber}{The chamber where propellant burns in a rocket engine.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-injector}\dictentry{Injector}{A device mixing fuel and oxidizer in the combustion chamber.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-nozzle}\dictentry{Nozzle}{A duct accelerating exhaust gases to produce thrust.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-de-laval-nozzle}\dictentry{De Laval Nozzle}{A convergent-divergent nozzle for supersonic exhaust.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-throat}\dictentry{Throat}{The narrowest section of a rocket nozzle.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-expansion-ratio}\dictentry{Expansion Ratio}{The ratio of nozzle exit area to throat area.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-nozzle-contour}\dictentry{Nozzle Contour}{The shape of a rocket nozzle wall.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-rao-nozzle}\dictentry{Rao Nozzle}{A nozzle contoured for maximum thrust.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-bell-nozzle}\dictentry{Bell Nozzle}{A curved contour nozzle for high altitude.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-plug-nozzle}\dictentry{Plug Nozzle}{An altitude-compensating nozzle design.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-aerospike-nozzle}\dictentry{Aerospike Nozzle}{A nozzle using external flow for altitude compensation.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-regenerative-cooling}\dictentry{Regenerative Cooling}{Cooling a nozzle by circulating propellant through it.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-film-cooling}\dictentry{Film Cooling}{Cooling by injecting a coolant film on the chamber wall.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-ablative-cooling}\dictentry{Ablative Cooling}{Cooling by ablating the inner surface material.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-radiative-cooling}\dictentry{Radiative Cooling}{Cooling by thermal radiation from the nozzle.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-igniter}\dictentry{Igniter}{A device initiating combustion in the chamber.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-pyrotechnic-igniter}\dictentry{Pyrotechnic Igniter}{An igniter using pyrotechnic compounds.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-spark-igniter}\dictentry{Spark Igniter}{An igniter using an electric spark.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-catalytic-igniter}\dictentry{Catalytic Igniter}{An igniter using a catalyst for hypergolic or monopropellant ignition.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-thrust-chamber}\dictentry{Thrust Chamber}{The assembly including combustion chamber and nozzle.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-engine-cycle}\dictentry{Engine Cycle}{The propellant feed cycle of a liquid rocket engine.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-pressure-fed-cycle}\dictentry{Pressure Fed Cycle}{An engine cycle using tank pressure for propellant feed.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-gas-generator-cycle}\dictentry{Gas Generator Cycle}{A cycle burning a small portion of propellant to drive a turbine.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-staged-combustion-cycle}\dictentry{Staged Combustion Cycle}{A cycle burning preburner exhaust in the main chamber.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-full-flow-staged-combustion}\dictentry{Full Flow Staged Combustion}{A staged combustion cycle with two preburners.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-expander-cycle}\dictentry{Expander Cycle}{A cycle using heat absorption to drive the turbine.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-tap-off-cycle}\dictentry{Tap-Off Cycle}{A cycle tapping combustion gas to drive the turbine.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-electric-pump-cycle}\dictentry{Electric Pump Cycle}{A cycle using electric motors to drive pumps.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-turbopump}\dictentry{Turbopump}{A turbine-driven pump feeding propellant to the engine.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-preburner}\dictentry{Preburner}{A small chamber generating gas to drive the turbine.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-main-combustion-chamber}\dictentry{Main Combustion Chamber}{The primary chamber where full combustion occurs.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-thrust-vector-control}\dictentry{Thrust Vector Control}{Steering a rocket by redirecting thrust.}{catRM}
\glsadd{D-gimbal}\dictentry{Gimbal}{A swivel mount for engine thrust vectoring.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-vernier-engine}\dictentry{Vernier Engine}{A small engine for fine trajectory adjustments.}{catRM}
\glsadd{SS-reaction-control-system}\dictentry{Reaction Control System}{Small thrusters for attitude control in space.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-cold-gas-thruster}\dictentry{Cold Gas Thruster}{A thruster using compressed gas for attitude control.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-launch-vehicle}\dictentry{Launch Vehicle}{A rocket used to launch payloads into space.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-expendable-launch-vehicle}\dictentry{Expendable Launch Vehicle}{A single-use launch vehicle.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-reusable-launch-vehicle}\dictentry{Reusable Launch Vehicle}{A launch vehicle with recoverable stages.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-multi-stage-rocket}\dictentry{Multi-Stage Rocket}{A rocket with multiple stages discarded during ascent.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-first-stage}\dictentry{First Stage}{The initial stage providing lift-off thrust.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-second-stage}\dictentry{Second Stage}{The stage igniting after first stage separation.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-upper-stage}\dictentry{Upper Stage}{The final stage for orbit insertion.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-strap-on-booster}\dictentry{Strap-On Booster}{An auxiliary booster attached to the first stage.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-payload-fairing}\dictentry{Payload Fairing}{The nose cone protecting the payload.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-interstage}\dictentry{Interstage}{A structure connecting rocket stages.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-stage-separation}\dictentry{Stage Separation}{The process of discarding an expended stage.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-fairing-separation}\dictentry{Fairing Separation}{Jettisoning the payload fairing after atmospheric exit.}{catRM}
\glsadd{SS-launch-pad}\dictentry{Launch Pad}{The facility from which a rocket launches.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-launch-tower}\dictentry{Launch Tower}{A structure supporting the rocket before launch.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-launch-rail}\dictentry{Launch Rail}{A rail guiding the rocket during initial ascent.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-launch-tube}\dictentry{Launch Tube}{A tube for launching guided missiles.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-mobile-launcher}\dictentry{Mobile Launcher}{A transportable launch platform.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-silo}\dictentry{Silo}{An underground launch facility for missiles.}{catRM}
\glsadd{SS-countdown}\dictentry{Countdown}{The timeline leading to launch.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-t-0}\dictentry{T-0}{The moment of engine ignition or lift-off.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-lift-off}\dictentry{Lift-Off}{The moment the rocket leaves the ground.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-max-q}\dictentry{Max Q}{The point of maximum dynamic pressure during ascent.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-meco}\dictentry{MECO}{Main engine cutoff.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-seco}\dictentry{SECO}{Second engine cutoff.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-insertion}\dictentry{Insertion}{The process of entering the intended orbit.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-injection-burn}\dictentry{Injection Burn}{A burn to inject into a transfer trajectory.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-circularization-burn}\dictentry{Circularization Burn}{A burn to circularize an orbit.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-deorbit-burn}\dictentry{Deorbit Burn}{A burn to initiate return to Earth.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-retro-rocket}\dictentry{Retro Rocket}{A rocket firing opposite to the direction of travel.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-ullage-motor}\dictentry{Ullage Motor}{A small motor settling propellant before main engine ignition.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-spin-motor}\dictentry{Spin Motor}{A motor spinning a stage for stability.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-separation-motor}\dictentry{Separation Motor}{A small motor separating stages.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-irfna}\dictentry{IRFNA}{Inhibited red fuming nitric acid, a storable oxidizer.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-lox}\dictentry{LOX}{Liquid oxygen.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-lh2}\dictentry{LH2}{Liquid hydrogen.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-lng}\dictentry{LNG}{Liquefied natural gas as rocket fuel.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-propellant-mass-fraction}\dictentry{Propellant Mass Fraction}{The ratio of propellant mass to total mass.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-dry-mass}\dictentry{Dry Mass}{The mass of the rocket without propellant.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-wet-mass}\dictentry{Wet Mass}{The mass of the rocket with propellant.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-payload-mass-fraction}\dictentry{Payload Mass Fraction}{The ratio of payload mass to total mass.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-tsiolkovsky-equation}\dictentry{Tsiolkovsky Equation}{The rocket equation: delta-v = ve * ln(m0/mf).}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-characteristic-velocity}\dictentry{Characteristic Velocity}{A measure of combustion performance (c*).}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-exhaust-velocity}\dictentry{Exhaust Velocity}{The speed of exhaust gases leaving the nozzle.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-effective-exhaust-velocity}\dictentry{Effective Exhaust Velocity}{The effective speed of exhaust including pressure effects.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-mass-flow-rate}\dictentry{Mass Flow Rate}{The mass of propellant flowing per unit time.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-chamber-pressure}\dictentry{Chamber Pressure}{The pressure inside the combustion chamber.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-expansion-ratio-effect}\dictentry{Expansion Ratio Effect}{The effect of nozzle expansion on performance.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-optimal-expansion}\dictentry{Optimal Expansion}{Nozzle expansion matching ambient pressure.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-overexpansion}\dictentry{Overexpansion}{Nozzle exit pressure below ambient.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-underexpansion}\dictentry{Underexpansion}{Nozzle exit pressure above ambient.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-flow-separation-in-nozzle}\dictentry{Flow Separation in Nozzle}{Detachment of flow from the nozzle wall.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-side-load}\dictentry{Side Load}{Lateral loads from flow separation in a nozzle.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-nozzle-extension}\dictentry{Nozzle Extension}{An extendable nozzle section for altitude adaptation.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-dual-bell-nozzle}\dictentry{Dual Bell Nozzle}{A nozzle with two bell contours for altitude compensation.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-thrust-chamber-liner}\dictentry{Thrust Chamber Liner}{A liner protecting the thrust chamber wall.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-combustion-stability}\dictentry{Combustion Stability}{Stable combustion without pressure oscillations.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-combustion-instability}\dictentry{Combustion Instability}{Pressure oscillations in the combustion chamber.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-chugging}\dictentry{Chugging}{Low-frequency combustion instability.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-buzzing}\dictentry{Buzzing}{Intermediate frequency instability.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-screaming}\dictentry{Screaming}{High-frequency combustion instability.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-pogo-oscillation}\dictentry{Pogo Oscillation}{Longitudinal oscillation from engine-vehicle interaction.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-acoustic-cavity}\dictentry{Acoustic Cavity}{A cavity damping acoustic modes in the chamber.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-baffle}\dictentry{Baffle}{A device in the injector to prevent instability.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-resonator}\dictentry{Resonator}{A device damping specific frequencies.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-injector-pattern}\dictentry{Injector Pattern}{The arrangement of injector elements.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-showerhead-injector}\dictentry{Showerhead Injector}{A simple injector with many small holes.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-impinging-injector}\dictentry{Impinging Injector}{An injector where jets impinge for mixing.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-coaxial-injector}\dictentry{Coaxial Injector}{An injector with concentric fuel and oxidizer streams.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-pintle-injector}\dictentry{Pintle Injector}{An injector with a central pintle for variable flow.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-shear-injector}\dictentry{Shear Injector}{An injector using shear for atomization.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-atomization}\dictentry{Atomization}{The breakup of propellant into fine droplets.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-droplet-combustion}\dictentry{Droplet Combustion}{Burning of individual propellant droplets.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-combustion-efficiency}\dictentry{Combustion Efficiency}{How completely propellant is burned.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-c-star-efficiency}\dictentry{C Star Efficiency}{Combustion efficiency measured by characteristic velocity.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-thrust-coefficient}\dictentry{Thrust Coefficient}{A dimensionless measure of nozzle performance.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-specific-impulse-efficiency}\dictentry{Specific Impulse Efficiency}{The achieved Isp as a fraction of theoretical.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-grain}\dictentry{Grain}{A shaped solid propellant charge.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-grain-configuration}\dictentry{Grain Configuration}{The geometric shape of the solid grain.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-burning-surface}\dictentry{Burning Surface}{The surface area of the grain that is burning.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-neutral-burning}\dictentry{Neutral Burning}{Burning with constant surface area.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-progressive-burning}\dictentry{Progressive Burning}{Burning with increasing surface area.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-regressive-burning}\dictentry{Regressive Burning}{Burning with decreasing surface area.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-end-burning}\dictentry{End Burning}{Burning from one end of the grain.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-internal-burning}\dictentry{Internal Burning}{Burning from internal passages.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-cigarette-burning}\dictentry{Cigarette Burning}{End burning of a solid grain.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-star-grain}\dictentry{Star Grain}{A grain with a star-shaped internal bore.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-wagon-wheel-grain}\dictentry{Wagon Wheel Grain}{A grain with a wagon wheel cross-section.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-slotted-tube-grain}\dictentry{Slotted Tube Grain}{A tubular grain with slots for burning surface control.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-inhibitor}\dictentry{Inhibitor}{A coating preventing burning on certain grain surfaces.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-propellant-liner}\dictentry{Propellant Liner}{A liner bonding the grain to the case.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-case}\dictentry{Case}{The pressure vessel containing the solid propellant.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-motor-case}\dictentry{Motor Case}{The structural casing of a solid rocket motor.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-insulation}\dictentry{Insulation}{Thermal protection inside the motor case.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-nozzle-throat-insert}\dictentry{Nozzle Throat Insert}{A high-temperature material at the nozzle throat.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-erosion}\dictentry{Erosion}{Material loss from the nozzle throat during firing.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-graphite-nozzle}\dictentry{Graphite Nozzle}{A nozzle using graphite for high temperature resistance.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-carbon-carbon-nozzle}\dictentry{Carbon-Carbon Nozzle}{A nozzle using carbon-carbon composite.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-metal-nozzle}\dictentry{Metal Nozzle}{A nozzle made of high-temperature metal.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-nozzle-closure}\dictentry{Nozzle Closure}{A seal protecting the nozzle before ignition.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-igniter-grain}\dictentry{Igniter Grain}{A small grain for motor ignition.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-pyrogen-igniter}\dictentry{Pyrogen Igniter}{A pyrotechnic igniter generating hot gas.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-safe-and-arm-device}\dictentry{Safe and Arm Device}{A safety device preventing accidental ignition.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-thrust-termination}\dictentry{Thrust Termination}{A system to shut down thrust.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-destruct-system}\dictentry{Destruct System}{A system destroying the rocket in flight.}{catRM}
\glsadd{D-flight-termination-system}\dictentry{Flight Termination System}{A system for range safety destruct.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-range-safety}\dictentry{Range Safety}{Protecting the public from launch hazards.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-missile}\dictentry{Missile}{A guided weapon system.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-ballistic-missile}\dictentry{Ballistic Missile}{A missile following a ballistic trajectory.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-cruise-missile}\dictentry{Cruise Missile}{A self-navigating missile with continuous thrust.}{catRM}

\glsadd{RM-anti-ship-missile}\dictentry{Anti-Ship Missile}{A missile targeting ships.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-hypersonic-missile}\dictentry{Hypersonic Missile}{A missile capable of speeds above Mach 5.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-hypersonic-glide-vehicle}\dictentry{Hypersonic Glide Vehicle}{A maneuverable hypersonic boost-glide weapon.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-mirv}\dictentry{MIRV}{Multiple independently targetable reentry vehicles.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-reentry-vehicle}\dictentry{Reentry Vehicle}{The part of a missile carrying the warhead through reentry.}{catRM}

\glsadd{RM-nuclear-warhead}\dictentry{Nuclear Warhead}{A nuclear explosive warhead.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-guidance-system}\dictentry{Guidance System}{The system directing a missile to its target.}{catRM}

\glsadd{RM-terrain-contour-matching}\dictentry{Terrain Contour Matching}{Guidance matching terrain data.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-scene-matching}\dictentry{Scene Matching}{Guidance matching imagery data.}{catRM}

\glsadd{RM-semi-active-radar-homing}\dictentry{Semi-Active Radar Homing}{Homing using reflected radar from an external illuminator.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-passive-radar-homing}\dictentry{Passive Radar Homing}{Homing using emissions from the target.}{catRM}

\glsadd{RM-laser-homing}\dictentry{Laser Homing}{Homing using reflected laser energy.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-command-guidance}\dictentry{Command Guidance}{Guidance from an external command link.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-beam-riding}\dictentry{Beam Riding}{Guidance following a directed beam.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-wire-guidance}\dictentry{Wire Guidance}{Guidance via wires spooled from the missile.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-fiber-optic-guidance}\dictentry{Fiber Optic Guidance}{Guidance via fiber optic link.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-terminal-guidance}\dictentry{Terminal Guidance}{The final phase of missile guidance.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-midcourse-guidance}\dictentry{Midcourse Guidance}{The intermediate phase of missile guidance.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-boost-phase}\dictentry{Boost Phase}{The initial phase of missile flight under power.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-coast-phase}\dictentry{Coast Phase}{The unpowered phase of missile flight.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-reentry-phase}\dictentry{Reentry Phase}{The final phase returning through the atmosphere.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-trajectory}\dictentry{Trajectory}{The path followed by a missile or rocket.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-ballistic-trajectory}\dictentry{Ballistic Trajectory}{A trajectory governed by gravity and inertia.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-lifting-trajectory}\dictentry{Lifting Trajectory}{A trajectory using aerodynamic lift.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-depressed-trajectory}\dictentry{Depressed Trajectory}{A flatter than normal ballistic trajectory.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-lofted-trajectory}\dictentry{Lofted Trajectory}{A higher than normal ballistic trajectory.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-minimum-energy-trajectory}\dictentry{Minimum Energy Trajectory}{The most efficient trajectory for a given range.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-maximum-range-trajectory}\dictentry{Maximum Range Trajectory}{The trajectory achieving the greatest range.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-launch-azimuth}\dictentry{Launch Azimuth}{The direction of launch measured from north.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-impact-point}\dictentry{Impact Point}{The point where the missile strikes.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-cep}\dictentry{CEP}{Circular error probable, a measure of accuracy.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-kill-probability}\dictentry{Kill Probability}{The probability of destroying the target.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-lethality}\dictentry{Lethality}{The damaging effect of a warhead.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-fragmentation-warhead}\dictentry{Fragmentation Warhead}{A warhead releasing fragments.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-shaped-charge}\dictentry{Shaped Charge}{A shaped explosive for armor penetration.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-kinetic-kill-vehicle}\dictentry{Kinetic Kill Vehicle}{A hit-to-kill interceptor.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-decoy}\dictentry{Decoy}{A device misleading enemy defenses.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-countermeasure}\dictentry{Countermeasure}{A system defeating enemy targeting.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-chaff}\dictentry{Chaff}{Radar-reflective material for decoying.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-flare}\dictentry{Flare}{A heat source decoying infrared seekers.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-electronic-countermeasure}\dictentry{Electronic Countermeasure}{Electronic jamming or deception.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-penetration-aid}\dictentry{Penetration Aid}{A device helping a missile penetrate defenses.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-anti-ballistic-missile}\dictentry{Anti-Ballistic Missile}{A missile intercepting ballistic missiles.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-interceptor}\dictentry{Interceptor}{A missile designed to destroy incoming threats.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-hit-to-kill}\dictentry{Hit-to-Kill}{Direct impact interception.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-proximity-fuse}\dictentry{Proximity Fuse}{A fuse detonating near the target.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-contact-fuse}\dictentry{Contact Fuse}{A fuse detonating on impact.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-time-fuse}\dictentry{Time Fuse}{A fuse detonating after a set time.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-airburst}\dictentry{Airburst}{An explosion in the air above the target.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-terminal-phase}\dictentry{Terminal Phase}{The final engagement phase of an interceptor.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-launch-detection}\dictentry{Launch Detection}{Detecting a missile launch by satellites or radar.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-tracking-radar}\dictentry{Tracking Radar}{Radar following a target.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-fire-control-radar}\dictentry{Fire Control Radar}{Radar directing weapons.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-phased-array-radar}\dictentry{Phased Array Radar}{A radar with electronic beam steering.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-early-warning-radar}\dictentry{Early Warning Radar}{Radar providing early threat detection.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-over-the-horizon-radar}\dictentry{Over-the-Horizon Radar}{Radar seeing beyond the horizon.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-satellite-warning-system}\dictentry{Satellite Warning System}{A satellite system detecting missile launches.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-ballistic-missile-defense}\dictentry{Ballistic Missile Defense}{A system defending against ballistic missiles.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-thaad}\dictentry{THAAD}{Terminal High Altitude Area Defense.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-patriot}\dictentry{Patriot}{A US surface-to-air missile system.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-aegis}\dictentry{Aegis}{A US naval combat system for missile defense.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-ground-based-interceptor}\dictentry{Ground-Based Interceptor}{A US ground-based missile interceptor.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-iron-dome}\dictentry{Iron Dome}{An Israeli short-range air defense system.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-david-sling}\dictentry{David Sling}{An Israeli medium-range air defense system.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-arrow}\dictentry{Arrow}{An Israeli anti-ballistic missile system.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-s-400}\dictentry{S-400}{A Russian long-range air defense system.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-s-500}\dictentry{S-500}{A Russian next-generation air defense system.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-ramjet-missile}\dictentry{Ramjet Missile}{A missile using a ramjet engine.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-scramjet-missile}\dictentry{Scramjet Missile}{A missile using a scramjet engine.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-solid-rocket-booster}\dictentry{Solid Rocket Booster}{A solid rocket used as a strap-on booster.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-dual-thrust-motor}\dictentry{Dual Thrust Motor}{A motor with two thrust levels.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-boost-sustain-motor}\dictentry{Boost-Sustain Motor}{A motor providing boost then sustained thrust.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-thrust-profile}\dictentry{Thrust Profile}{The variation of thrust over burn time.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-flame-deflector}\dictentry{Flame Deflector}{A structure deflecting exhaust at launch.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-exhaust-plume}\dictentry{Exhaust Plume}{The visible exhaust of a rocket engine.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-mach-diamond}\dictentry{Mach Diamond}{Diamond-shaped shock patterns in exhaust.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-sounding-rocket}\dictentry{Sounding Rocket}{A suborbital rocket for research.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-meteorological-rocket}\dictentry{Meteorological Rocket}{A rocket for atmospheric measurements.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-target-drone}\dictentry{Target Drone}{An unmanned target used for missile testing.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-burn-rate}\dictentry{Burn Rate}{The rate at which solid propellant burns.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-strand-burn-rate}\dictentry{Strand Burn Rate}{Burn rate measured on a small sample.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-exhaust-trail}\dictentry{Exhaust Trail}{The visible trail from a missile exhaust.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-liquid-injection-thrust-vectoring}\dictentry{Liquid Injection Thrust Vectoring}{Injection of liquid into the nozzle for thrust vectoring.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-jet-vanes}\dictentry{Jet Vanes}{Vanes in the exhaust for steering.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-jet-tab}\dictentry{Jet Tab}{A tab in the exhaust for steering.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-flexible-bearing}\dictentry{Flexible Bearing}{A flexible joint for nozzle gimballing.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-hot-gas-valve}\dictentry{Hot Gas Valve}{A valve directing hot gas for thrust vectoring.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-secondary-injection}\dictentry{Secondary Injection}{Injection of secondary fluid for thrust vectoring.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-main-propellant-valve}\dictentry{Main Propellant Valve}{The valve controlling propellant flow to the engine.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-fill-valve}\dictentry{Fill Valve}{A valve for filling propellant tanks.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-drain-valve}\dictentry{Drain Valve}{A valve for draining propellant.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-vent-valve}\dictentry{Vent Valve}{A valve for tank venting.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-relief-valve}\dictentry{Relief Valve}{A valve preventing overpressure.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-check-valve}\dictentry{Check Valve}{A valve allowing flow in one direction only.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-solenoid-valve}\dictentry{Solenoid Valve}{An electrically actuated valve.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-poppet-valve}\dictentry{Poppet Valve}{A valve with a disc and seat.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-ball-valve}\dictentry{Ball Valve}{A valve with a rotating ball.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-butterfly-valve}\dictentry{Butterfly Valve}{A valve with a rotating disc.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-pneumatic-valve}\dictentry{Pneumatic Valve}{A valve actuated by compressed gas.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-pyro-valve}\dictentry{Pyro Valve}{A valve actuated by pyrotechnic charge.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-propellant-line}\dictentry{Propellant Line}{A line carrying propellant.}{catRM}

\glsadd{RM-discharge-line}\dictentry{Discharge Line}{A line on the outlet side of the pump.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-helium-pressurization}\dictentry{Helium Pressurization}{Pressurization using helium.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-nitrogen-pressurization}\dictentry{Nitrogen Pressurization}{Pressurization using nitrogen.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-autogenous-pressurization}\dictentry{Autogenous Pressurization}{Pressurization using vaporized propellant.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-accumulator}\dictentry{Accumulator}{A pressure storage reservoir.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-pressure-regulator}\dictentry{Pressure Regulator}{A device maintaining constant pressure.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-gas-generator}\dictentry{Gas Generator}{A device generating gas to drive a turbine.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-heat-exchanger}\dictentry{Heat Exchanger}{A device transferring heat between fluids.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-gas-cooler}\dictentry{Gas Cooler}{A device cooling gas before use.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-filter}\dictentry{Filter}{A device removing particles from propellant.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-propellant-management}\dictentry{Propellant Management}{The system managing propellant in tanks.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-propellant-settling}\dictentry{Propellant Settling}{Using small thrusters to settle propellant.}{catRM}

\glsadd{RM-spin-stabilized-rocket}\dictentry{Spin-Stabilized Rocket}{A rocket stabilized by rotating around its longitudinal axis.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-tail-fins}\dictentry{Tail Fins}{Aerodynamic surfaces on the rear of a rocket for stability.}{catRM}
\glsadd{RM-canard-control}\dictentry{Canard Control}{Forward-mounted control surfaces for missile maneuvering.}{catRM}

\clearpage
\chapter*{Drones}
\addcontentsline{toc}{chapter}{Drones}
\markboth{Drones}{Drones}
\clearpage
\begin{multicols}{2}
\small
Unmanned aerial vehicles: multirotor and fixed-wing drones, flight controllers, autonomy, regulations, and the technology powering the UAV revolution.\par
\vspace{0.5cm}
\glsadd{D-drone}\dictentry{Drone}{An unmanned aerial vehicle operated remotely or autonomously.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-uav}\dictentry{UAV}{Unmanned Aerial Vehicle, the aircraft component of a UAS.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-uas}\dictentry{UAS}{Unmanned Aircraft System including vehicle, ground control, and data link.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-rpas}\dictentry{RPAS}{Remotely Piloted Aircraft System.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-multirotor}\dictentry{Multirotor}{A drone with multiple rotors, typically 4, 6, or 8.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-quadcopter}\dictentry{Quadcopter}{A drone with four rotors arranged in a cross configuration.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-hexacopter}\dictentry{Hexacopter}{A drone with six rotors for redundancy and lift capacity.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-octocopter}\dictentry{Octocopter}{A drone with eight rotors for maximum redundancy.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-fixed-wing-drone}\dictentry{Fixed-Wing Drone}{A drone with a rigid wing for efficient forward flight.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-vtol-drone}\dictentry{VTOL Drone}{A drone capable of vertical takeoff and landing with fixed-wing efficiency.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-hybrid-drone}\dictentry{Hybrid Drone}{A drone combining multirotor and fixed-wing configurations.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-nano-drone}\dictentry{Nano Drone}{A very small drone, typically under 250 grams.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-micro-drone}\dictentry{Micro Drone}{A small drone, usually under 2 kg.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-tactical-drone}\dictentry{Tactical Drone}{A mid-range drone for battlefield or operational use.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-male-drone}\dictentry{MALE Drone}{Medium Altitude Long Endurance drone between 10,000 and 30,000 feet.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-hale-drone}\dictentry{HALE Drone}{High Altitude Long Endurance drone above 30,000 feet.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-combat-drone}\dictentry{Combat Drone}{Armed drone designed for military strike missions.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-reconnaissance-drone}\dictentry{Reconnaissance Drone}{A drone designed for intelligence gathering.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-swarm-drone}\dictentry{Swarm Drone}{A drone designed to operate as part of a coordinated group.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-delivery-drone}\dictentry{Delivery Drone}{A drone designed for parcel and cargo delivery.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-agriculture-drone}\dictentry{Agriculture Drone}{A drone optimized for crop monitoring and spraying.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-surveying-drone}\dictentry{Surveying Drone}{A drone equipped for mapping and land surveying.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-inspection-drone}\dictentry{Inspection Drone}{A drone used for infrastructure inspection.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-search-and-rescue-drone}\dictentry{Search and Rescue Drone}{A drone with thermal cameras and sensors for SAR operations.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-racing-drone}\dictentry{Racing Drone}{A high-speed drone designed for competitive FPV racing.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-fpv-drone}\dictentry{FPV Drone}{First Person View drone piloted via onboard camera feed.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-cinematic-drone}\dictentry{Cinematic Drone}{A drone with high-quality cameras for filmmaking.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-submersible-drone}\dictentry{Submersible Drone}{An underwater drone for marine inspection.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-autonomous-drone}\dictentry{Autonomous Drone}{A drone capable of self-navigation without pilot input.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-tethered-drone}\dictentry{Tethered Drone}{A drone powered via a cable for unlimited endurance.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-solar-drone}\dictentry{Solar Drone}{A drone powered by solar panels.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-flight-controller}\dictentry{Flight Controller}{The onboard computer managing drone flight dynamics and stability.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-imu}\dictentry{IMU}{Inertial Measurement Unit combining accelerometers and gyroscopes.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-accelerometer}\dictentry{Accelerometer}{A sensor measuring linear acceleration along three axes.}{catD}
\glsadd{SS-gyroscope}\dictentry{Gyroscope}{A sensor measuring angular velocity about three axes.}{catD}
\glsadd{SS-magnetometer}\dictentry{Magnetometer}{A sensor measuring magnetic field for heading reference.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-barometer}\dictentry{Barometer}{A sensor measuring atmospheric pressure for altitude hold.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-gps-module}\dictentry{GPS Module}{A receiver providing global position data for navigation.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-glonass}\dictentry{GLONASS}{Russian satellite navigation system used alongside GPS.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-compass}\dictentry{Compass}{A magnetic heading sensor for drone orientation.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-esc}\dictentry{ESC}{Electronic Speed Controller driving the drone's motors.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-motor}\dictentry{Motor}{The electric motor powering a drone rotor.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-brushless-motor}\dictentry{Brushless Motor}{An efficient, durable motor commonly used in drones.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-propeller}\dictentry{Propeller}{The rotating blade producing thrust on a drone.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-pitch-propeller}\dictentry{Pitch Propeller}{A propeller designed for clockwise rotation.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-yaw-propeller}\dictentry{Yaw Propeller}{Counter-rotating propellers cancel torque for stable flight.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-lipo-battery}\dictentry{LiPo Battery}{Lithium Polymer battery used for drone power.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-battery-capacity}\dictentry{Battery Capacity}{Battery energy measured in milliampere-hours (mAh).}{catD}
\glsadd{D-s-rating}\dictentry{S Rating}{Number of cells in series in a LiPo battery.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-c-rating}\dictentry{C Rating}{Discharge rate rating of a LiPo battery.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-power-distribution-board}\dictentry{Power Distribution Board}{A board distributing battery power to the ESCs.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-pdb}\dictentry{PDB}{Power Distribution Board.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-bec}\dictentry{BEC}{Battery Eliminator Circuit providing regulated voltage.}{catD}
\glsadd{SS-telemetry}\dictentry{Telemetry}{Real-time data transmission from drone to ground station.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-receiver}\dictentry{Receiver}{The radio receiver on the drone for pilot commands.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-transmitter}\dictentry{Transmitter}{The radio transmitter used by the pilot to control the drone.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-radio-link}\dictentry{Radio Link}{The communication channel between transmitter and drone.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-control-link}\dictentry{Control Link}{The uplink for sending commands to the drone.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-video-link}\dictentry{Video Link}{The downlink transmitting camera video to the ground.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-data-link}\dictentry{Data Link}{A bi-directional link for telemetry and commands.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-frequency-band}\dictentry{Frequency Band}{The radio frequency used for drone communication.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-2.4-ghz}\dictentry{2.4 GHz}{Common control frequency band for RC systems.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-5.8-ghz}\dictentry{5.8 GHz}{Common frequency band for FPV video transmission.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-900-mhz}\dictentry{900 MHz}{A longer-range frequency band for telemetry.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-433-mhz}\dictentry{433 MHz}{An ultra-long-range frequency band.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-fhss}\dictentry{FHSS}{Frequency Hopping Spread Spectrum, a robust modulation method.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-dsss}\dictentry{DSSS}{Direct Sequence Spread Spectrum modulation.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-lbt}\dictentry{LBT}{Listen Before Talk, a spectrum access protocol.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-rssi}\dictentry{RSSI}{Received Signal Strength Indicator for link quality.}{catD}
\glsadd{SS-antenna}\dictentry{Antenna}{A device transmitting or receiving radio signals.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-omnidirectional-antenna}\dictentry{Omnidirectional Antenna}{An antenna radiating equally in all directions.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-directional-antenna}\dictentry{Directional Antenna}{An antenna focusing signal in one direction.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-patch-antenna}\dictentry{Patch Antenna}{A flat directional antenna.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-yagi-antenna}\dictentry{Yagi Antenna}{A high-gain directional antenna.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-gimbal}\dictentry{Gimbal}{A stabilizing mount for cameras and sensors.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-3-axis-gimbal}\dictentry{3-Axis Gimbal}{A gimbal stabilizing pitch, roll, and yaw.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-2-axis-gimbal}\dictentry{2-Axis Gimbal}{A gimbal stabilizing pitch and roll.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-pid-tuning}\dictentry{PID Tuning}{Adjusting PID controller gains for optimal flight performance.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-autotune}\dictentry{Autotune}{Automatic PID tuning by the flight controller.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-rate-mode}\dictentry{Rate Mode}{A flight mode controlling angular rates directly.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-angle-mode}\dictentry{Angle Mode}{A flight mode limiting tilt angle for stable flight.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-horizon-mode}\dictentry{Horizon Mode}{A flight mode with both rate and angle characteristics.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-altitude-hold}\dictentry{Altitude Hold}{A mode maintaining a constant barometric altitude.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-position-hold}\dictentry{Position Hold}{A mode maintaining GPS position and altitude.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-return-to-home}\dictentry{Return to Home}{A mode automatically flying the drone back to its launch point.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-rth}\dictentry{RTH}{Return to Home.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-loiter}\dictentry{Loiter}{A mode holding position and altitude.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-waypoint-navigation}\dictentry{Waypoint Navigation}{Autonomous flight through programmed GPS waypoints.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-follow-me}\dictentry{Follow Me}{A mode following a GPS-tracked person or device.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-orbit-mode}\dictentry{Orbit Mode}{A mode circling around a point of interest.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-mission-planning}\dictentry{Mission Planning}{Creating an autonomous flight plan with waypoints and actions.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-geofence}\dictentry{Geofence}{A virtual boundary restricting the drone's flight area.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-low-battery-warning}\dictentry{Low Battery Warning}{An alert when battery voltage drops below a threshold.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-collision-avoidance}\dictentry{Collision Avoidance}{A system detecting and avoiding obstacles.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-obstacle-detection}\dictentry{Obstacle Detection}{Sensing obstacles using cameras, LiDAR, or ultrasonic sensors.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-emergency-stop}\dictentry{Emergency Stop}{An immediate motor kill command.}{catD}
\glsadd{D-lost-link}\dictentry{Lost Link}{Loss of communication between the transmitter and drone.}{catD}

\clearpage
\chapter*{Space Science}
\addcontentsline{toc}{chapter}{Space Science}
\markboth{Space Science}{Space Science}
\clearpage
\begin{multicols}{2}
\small
Orbital mechanics, spacecraft engineering, and the space environment. From LEO to Lagrange points, re-entry to radiation belts.\par
\vspace{0.5cm}
\glsadd{SS-space-science}\dictentry{Space Science}{The scientific study of the space environment, celestial bodies, and the universe.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-astronomy}\dictentry{Astronomy}{The study of celestial objects, space, and the universe as a whole.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-astrophysics}\dictentry{Astrophysics}{The branch of astronomy dealing with the physics of celestial objects.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-cosmology}\dictentry{Cosmology}{The study of the origin, evolution, and structure of the universe.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-planetary-science}\dictentry{Planetary Science}{The study of planets, moons, and planetary systems.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-exoplanet}\dictentry{Exoplanet}{A planet orbiting a star outside our solar system.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-solar-system}\dictentry{Solar System}{The Sun and all objects gravitationally bound to it.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-star}\dictentry{Star}{A luminous sphere of plasma held together by gravity.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-planet}\dictentry{Planet}{A large celestial body orbiting a star.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-moon}\dictentry{Moon}{A natural satellite orbiting a planet.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-asteroid}\dictentry{Asteroid}{A small rocky body orbiting the Sun.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-comet}\dictentry{Comet}{An icy body releasing gas and dust when near the Sun.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-meteor}\dictentry{Meteor}{A streak of light from a meteoroid burning in the atmosphere.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-meteorite}\dictentry{Meteorite}{A meteoroid that reaches the Earth's surface.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-meteoroid}\dictentry{Meteoroid}{A small rocky or metallic body in space.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-asteroid-belt}\dictentry{Asteroid Belt}{A region between Mars and Jupiter containing many asteroids.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-kuiper-belt}\dictentry{Kuiper Belt}{A region beyond Neptune with icy bodies and dwarf planets.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-oort-cloud}\dictentry{Oort Cloud}{A distant spherical shell of icy bodies surrounding the solar system.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-dwarf-planet}\dictentry{Dwarf Planet}{A celestial body in hydrostatic equilibrium orbiting the Sun.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-pluto}\dictentry{Pluto}{A dwarf planet in the Kuiper Belt.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-ceres}\dictentry{Ceres}{The largest asteroid and a dwarf planet in the asteroid belt.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-sun}\dictentry{Sun}{The star at the center of our solar system.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-solar-flare}\dictentry{Solar Flare}{A sudden eruption of energy on the Sun's surface.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-coronal-mass-ejection}\dictentry{Coronal Mass Ejection}{A massive burst of solar wind and magnetic fields.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-solar-wind}\dictentry{Solar Wind}{A stream of charged particles released from the Sun.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-heliosphere}\dictentry{Heliosphere}{The region of space dominated by the solar wind.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-magnetosphere}\dictentry{Magnetosphere}{The region around a planet influenced by its magnetic field.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-van-allen-belt}\dictentry{Van Allen Belt}{A zone of energetic charged particles trapped by Earth's magnetic field.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-aurora}\dictentry{Aurora}{A natural light display from solar wind interactions with the atmosphere.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-northern-lights}\dictentry{Northern Lights}{Aurora Borealis in the northern hemisphere.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-southern-lights}\dictentry{Southern Lights}{Aurora Australis in the southern hemisphere.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-ionosphere}\dictentry{Ionosphere}{The ionized layer of the upper atmosphere.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-atmosphere}\dictentry{Atmosphere}{The layer of gases surrounding a planet.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-exosphere}\dictentry{Exosphere}{The outermost layer of the atmosphere.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-thermosphere}\dictentry{Thermosphere}{The atmosphere layer above the mesosphere with rising temperature.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-mesosphere}\dictentry{Mesosphere}{The atmosphere layer above the stratosphere.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-stratosphere}\dictentry{Stratosphere}{The atmosphere layer above the troposphere containing the ozone layer.}{catSS}

\glsadd{SS-ozone-layer}\dictentry{Ozone Layer}{A stratospheric layer absorbing UV radiation.}{catSS}

\glsadd{SS-integration-facility}\dictentry{Integration Facility}{A building for spacecraft and rocket integration.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-payload-processing}\dictentry{Payload Processing}{Preparing the payload for launch.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-propellant-loading}\dictentry{Propellant Loading}{Fuelling the spacecraft or stage.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-countdown}\dictentry{Countdown}{The timeline before launch.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-big-bang}\dictentry{Big Bang}{The cosmological event that began the expansion of the universe.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-dark-matter}\dictentry{Dark Matter}{An unseen form of matter inferred from its gravitational effects.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-dark-energy}\dictentry{Dark Energy}{A mysterious force causing the accelerated expansion of the universe.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-black-hole}\dictentry{Black Hole}{An object with gravity so strong that nothing, not even light, can escape.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-neutron-star}\dictentry{Neutron Star}{An extremely dense stellar remnant composed primarily of neutrons.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-white-dwarf}\dictentry{White Dwarf}{A stellar remnant supported by electron degeneracy pressure.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-supernova}\dictentry{Supernova}{A catastrophic stellar explosion that can outshine an entire galaxy.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-nebula}\dictentry{Nebula}{An interstellar cloud of dust, hydrogen, helium, and plasma.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-galaxy}\dictentry{Galaxy}{A massive system of stars, stellar remnants, gas, dust, and dark matter.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-milky-way}\dictentry{Milky Way}{The galaxy containing our Solar System.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-light-year}\dictentry{Light-Year}{The distance light travels in one year, about 9.46 trillion km.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-astronomical-unit}\dictentry{Astronomical Unit}{The mean distance from Earth to the Sun, about 150 million km.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-parsec}\dictentry{Parsec}{A unit of distance equal to about 3.26 light-years.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-redshift}\dictentry{Redshift}{The stretching of light to longer wavelengths due to cosmic expansion.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-cosmic-microwave-background}\dictentry{Cosmic Microwave Background}{The relic radiation from the Big Bang, now at microwave wavelengths.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-terrestrial-planet}\dictentry{Terrestrial Planet}{A rocky planet composed primarily of silicate rocks and metals.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-gas-giant}\dictentry{Gas Giant}{A large planet composed mostly of hydrogen and helium.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-impact-crater}\dictentry{Impact Crater}{A depression on a celestial body caused by a meteoroid impact.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-regolith}\dictentry{Regolith}{The loose, unconsolidated surface material covering solid celestial bodies.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-cubesat}\dictentry{Cubesat}{A standardized small satellite format, typically 10 cm per unit.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-smallsat}\dictentry{SmallSat}{A category of small satellites with reduced mass and size.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-space-tourism}\dictentry{Space Tourism}{Commercial spaceflights for recreational, leisure, or business purposes.}{catSS}
\glsadd{SS-planetary-protection}\dictentry{Planetary Protection}{Preventing biological contamination of celestial bodies during exploration.}{catSS}
\end{multicols}

\clearpage
